營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持南亞(1303)之投資建議為買進，主要考量：(1)南亞 7
月自結稅後 EPS 0.4 元，雖不如 6 月自結稅後 EPS 2.88 元，但因
6 月有包含權益法認列收益不具可比性，而具可比性的 4、5、7 月
自結稅前淨利率分別是 9.4%、13%、15.7%，歸母淨利率為 4.7%、
9.2%、10.4%，獲利顯著持續提升，符合研究部預期。營收方面，
7+8 月營收達成率 70.6%，優於預期，公司展望 9 月營收將優於 8
月，4Q26 的營收將優於 3Q26，整體營運維持正向。(2)應用於 AI
伺服器的 M6-M8 高階大訂單目前正與客戶洽談生產配合及廠區產
線對接等細節，預計將於 4Q26 完成協商並開始有明顯進展，帶動
後續營收與利潤大幅優化，去瓶頸後的 30 萬張 CCL 產能將全數投
入高階產品生產。(3)廠商持續將擴產資源湧入中高階材料，導致
以 PCB 為主軸的玻纖布、銅箔、CCL、ABF 載板等中低階電子材料
依然緊缺，研究部預期南亞電子材料漲價態勢將延續。此外，南亞
積極推動 AI 供應鏈高階材料的開發、認證與銷售，有顯著成效，
2027 年電子相關獲利占比也將提升至 98%，電子材料占比可望達
70-80%，優於研究部原先預估的65-70%。(4)子公司方面，受惠AI
需求旺盛 ， 南電產能有限但需求持續放大 ，ABF 載板漲價效應延續 ，
2026、2027 年業績皆會有明顯提升；南亞科受惠記憶體供需缺口
再擴大，利潤急速成長。(5)近期石化產品 EG 價格大漲，利差顯著
擴大，對獲利明顯幫助，若市況持續好轉，更明顯的獲利貢獻會落
在 4Q26。研究部分別上修南亞 2026 年、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 13.82
元、21.64 元(原估 12.02 元、16.94 元)，分別+2,325%YoY、
+56.6%YoY，年底淨值 69.8 元、91.4 元。研究部認為 2027 年 CSP
業者對整機成本及零組件價格控管趨嚴 ， 加上上游原料新增產能於
2H27 至1H28 陸續開出，電子材料及CCL 業者議價能力會降低，因
此將電子材料目標評價調降至 20 倍，惟考量目前股價隱含電子材
料評價僅約 12 倍，評價極低，故維持南亞買進投資建議。以2027
年電子材料:南亞科:傳產獲利占比 48:50:2，電子與傳產淨值占比
約 4：6 之比例推算，目標價調升至320 元(20xPER*2027 本業電子
材料 EPS + 5xPER*2027 南亞科貢獻稅後 EPS + 1xPBR*2027 傳產
BVPS)。

重點摘要： 
1. 南亞產品線包括在石化、塑膠、纖維與電子類，分佈廣泛

圖 1、南亞 1H26 部門別營收比重 
電子材料
52.7%
化工產品
16.9%
聚酯產品
15.8%
塑膠加工
12.7%
其他
1.9%

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞是全球最大塑膠加工廠，此外，其電子級玻纖絲為全球
第二大 ， 銅箔基板 、 玻纖布為全球第三大 ， 乙二醇EG為全球第
四大，環氧樹脂、丙二酚BPA為全球第五大業者。南亞產能全
球布局，主要生產基地在台灣，有57個廠， 中國有34個， 美國
有8個廠，共99個生產廠。

同時，南亞持有 26.3%的南亞科為全球第四大 DRAM廠，持有
61.4%的子公司南電為 「PCB三雄」 之一 ， 穩居全球前五大IC載
板廠，也是全球前十大PCB製造商。南亞的產品線為台塑集團
中最廣泛，分佈在石化、 纖維 、塑膠加工及電子材料等，1H26
電子材料佔比由 2025年的46.4% 提升到52.7%，化工產品由
21.8%降至16.9%，聚酯產品由16%略降至15.8%，塑膠產品由
13.9%降至12.7%， 其他1.9%。 公司生產的原材料多為集團內部
自用 ， 僅約少部分對外銷售 ， 外售若對公司更有幫助的話 ， 公
司將會提高外售比率。

聚酯產品包括聚酯薄膜、聚酯纖維(如聚酯棉、POY、DTY以及
瓶用酯粒)等 。 電子材料產品包括銅箔 、 銅箔基板(CCL)、 玻纖
絲、玻纖布、環氧樹脂(EPOXY)等；塑膠產品包括膠布、膠皮
與PU合成皮等。銷售地區廣泛，以中國、美國、台灣為主。

2. 子公司南電、南亞科漲價趨勢延續，母公司南亞間接受惠 
由於AI應用需求大增 ，ASIC需求預估在2026年大幅增加 ， 此類
產品全都用ABF載板 ，ABF載板於4Q25起漲 ，1Q26及2Q26皆維持
續漲價節奏。研究部認為接下來ABF廠議價能力轉強，南電報
價得以持續上漲，母公司南亞間接受惠。

南亞科2Q26持續繳出亮眼的成績，GM及OPM分別達到79.5%及
73.7%的水準 ， 不亞於一線大廠 ， 單季稅後EPS 14.66元 。 南亞
科預期3Q26營運及OPM將進一步提升 ， 且短缺情況至4Q26未見
變化 ， 同時預期市場在2028年前不會發生反轉 ， 母公司南亞間
接受惠。

股權變動方面，董事會之前決議處分南電 3%與南亞科1%的股
權 ， 其中 ， 南電的第一個3%已經處分完畢 ， 後來董事會又通過
再處分3%， 大部分已於2Q26底處分完畢 ， 目前沒有新的處分計
畫，兩次重大處分後仍持有南電61%股權。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

2026/3/25南亞科董事會宣布晟碟 （Sandisk） 、 鎧俠 （Kioxia）、
SK Hynix旗下的Solidigm、思科（Cisco）四家公司分三次私
募認購普通股共3.5億股 ， 股權稀釋約11.3%,研究部推算目前
南亞持有南亞科之持股比例約為26.3%。 而南亞持有南亞科的
3,099萬股權處分目前尚未執行，年底前也不一定會處分，預
估執行後持股比例將調整至25.4%。

3. 電子材料營收自1H26的52.7%提升至9月的60%以上

表1、電子材料為南亞獲利主體(營業利益佔比) 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

缺貨造成電子材料持續漲價，2025年初以來最先出現明顯供
給吃緊的玻纖 ， 近期已擴散至銅箔 、 銅箔基板等材料 ， 各材料
缺貨、漲價還會延續一段時間。中低階產品缺貨主因在於高
階化帶來的產能排擠，隨著玻纖、銅箔及基板高階產品製程
精度提升，生產速度下降，不論銅箔、玻纖、基板，要生產1
單位的高階材料，都必須放棄生產很多單位的中低階材料，
導致缺貨更嚴重，此情況將維持未來一段時間。

電子材料持續成為南亞營運轉型核心，產線維持高稼動率，
銅箔基板、玻纖布與玻纖絲產能利用率達滿載，銅箔與環氧
樹脂產能利用率在8-9成之間。8月電子材料營收占比已提升
至59.8%，較7月增加2.8pct， 正式逼近六成 。 電子材料產業到
2028、2029年仍可維持榮景。

業界有開始擴產，但擴產速度本身有限制，且擴產後也還是
會面臨材料的問題，此外，PCB層數大幅增加，將使得供給增
加，幅度仍跟不上需求的增速 。 整體而言 ， 電子材料供需偏緊
與漲價趨勢短期難以扭轉 ， 銅箔基板及高階PCB的浪潮會有望
延續。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

4. 玻纖獲利率最高，獲利貢獻CCL最大，全品項OPM持續提升 
表 2、南亞各產品別 OPM 逐季轉佳 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞產品價格漲幅因客戶依賴度而異，依客戶每個月機動調
整 。 由於中高階產品供不應求 ， 加上同業將產能轉做高階 ， 導
致一般消費性電子用的E-Glass產能吃緊 ， 這對產品報價相當
有利，報價自2025年開始每個月約調漲5-10%。

獲利率方面，現階段電子材料獲利率以玻纖紗/布排行第一、
近期CCL跟載板漲幅度大，獲利率已逐漸接近玻纖，排名升至
第二，再來是銅箔，而環氧樹脂有8成為非電子用途，因此獲
利率較銅箔低些。

獲利金額貢獻方面，雖然目前獲利率最高的是玻纖紗/布，但
CCL營收規模較大，所以獲利貢獻金額超過玻纖。

持續漲價對3Q26、4Q26利潤率是很重要因素，但除了漲價之
外 ， 產能利用率拉高 ， 固定成本不變 ， 因此訂單及出貨量增加
對利潤率也有一定貢獻， 產品組合改變也很重要， 整體來說 ，
電子材料全產品項的OPM皆持續提升當中。

電子材料產品主要包括： 
I.玻纖布 
南亞的玻纖布產能排名為全球第三，佔南亞的營收提升至 6-
7%，但獲利貢獻位居前列。年產能約5.9億米(以一般布計)，
自用比重原為40%-50%， 但為優先確保下游高階CCL供料無虞 ，
外銷比例已調降至30%-40%(自用仍以市價計價，不影響實質
利潤)，稼動率自6月起持續滿載。受全球嚴重缺料帶動，7-9
月玻纖布價格強勁上揚，預期4Q26動能不減。玻纖布/紗獲利
率為電子材料中最高，2Q26 OPM已達30%-40%以上。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

日系知名同業大廠為玻纖紗 /布領導廠商，將專注於高階市
場，而Low DK1玻纖布市場供不應求 ， 進而尋找南亞進行合作 。
此日本同業大廠提供玻纖紗，而南亞可提供Low DK1產能，製
成玻纖布後再回送日廠做後段處理。

此外，日本同業大廠也有供應Low DK2玻纖紗給南亞，織成布
後製成自家CCL基板可對外銷售，Low DK2 產品預計2026年內
會量產 ， 屬長期技術互惠合作 。 初期量不大 ， 但3Q26起日東紡
供貨量將明顯增加，足以支撐公司M8以上高階基板需求，對
於公司後續CCL發展，以及高階玻纖數量擴大會有一定幫助。

E-Glass部分 ， 南亞表示各類電子與消費型載板 、PCB層數持續
升級，即使終端消費總量持平，對E-Glass的用料需求依然持
續擴大。E-Glass自2025年底起漲勢確立，3Q26價格仍持續上
漲，公司在9月會議中表示，中國同業於年中報價約2.2美元/
米，4Q26期貨已漲至2.5美元/米 ， 公司預期2027年有機會上看
3美元/米，漲價動能依然非常強勁。南亞E-Glass採大窯爐生
產，與特殊玻纖的坩堝製程不衝突。

同業因原料緊缺或利潤考量放棄中低階訂單，客戶大量轉向
原料掌握度極高的南亞，使南亞在中階與消費型基板 (如M4
等)的稼動率維持滿載 ， 南亞不會放棄E-Glass供應 ， 未來將憑
藉原料自給優勢，承接大量同業外溢轉單，充分享受價量齊
揚帶來的超額利潤。

特殊玻纖紗使用白金坩堝設備，包含坩堝設計、冷卻拉絲等
環節有許多細微變化，會影響產品的介電常數。全球高階豐
田織布機排單已至2028-2029年 ， 南亞因為有超前部署織布機
產能 ， 提早下訂排單 ， 自4Q26起每年均有新機台如期到位加入
生產。透過台灣新港與昆山廠增設坩堝擴產，2026-2028年兩
岸累計將新增達100多個紡位。

高階石英布（Q-Glass）南亞在技術上仍需突破，初期石英紗
會先從外部供應商取得 ， 但因為公司具備生產玻纖紗的經驗 ，
目前還沒放棄，持續積極投入自主研發。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

II.銅箔 
南亞的銅箔產能排名全球第二，近期銅箔佔南亞營收已超過
10%。銅箔年產能約為11萬多噸，目前產能利用率滿載，自用
比例約50%。公司計畫在惠州擴建銅箔產線，將增加2.34萬噸
產能 ， 約擴張2成 ， 預計於2028年1月投產 。 台灣彰濱地區也有
銅箔擴產規劃，主要針對PCB應用為主。

南亞的銅箔目前銷量以HVLP1/2為主 ，HVLP3穩定量產 ， 且具備
HVLP 3.5(介於3與4之間的自有規格)及小量HVLP 4生產能力 ，
另外也有一些特定規格之超薄銅箔已進入量產，HVLP 5 則是
持續開發當中，產品線正積極朝向高階發展。

AI伺服器對超低損耗要求嚴格，高階銅箔結晶晶粒需極細，
製程具物理極限使生產速度大幅變慢，投下相同資源，高階
產出噸數遠低於傳統規格，產生嚴重產能排擠效應。

加工費方面，過去多逐季協商、具價格僵固性， 但自7、8月起
開始出現密集且連續調漲 。3Q26大宗銅箔每噸售價約1.7-1.8
萬美元(含銅價約1.4萬)，加工費約3-4千美元/噸。因結構性
生產降速與高階需求不可逆，預期加工費將具備永久性上升
動能，後續極有機會突破4-5千美元/噸。

III.銅箔基板(CCL) 
南亞的銅箔基板 （CCL） 產能排名全球第三 ， 目前CCL兩岸產能
約618萬張/月，台灣 ：中國崑山與惠州約3：7， 目前稼動率滿
載，公司表示1H26 M4 以上基板銷量逾200萬張。台灣樹林與
新港廠區既有設備的小幅改造去瓶頸進度超前，已提前於
4Q26初實現30萬張產能提升 ， 約+5%YoY， 將全數投入M7以上高
階訂單。

CCL佔南亞的營收比重已於 7月達30%左右，M系列佔其中的
2/3(約整體營收20%)，M4佔M系列的營收60%(約CCL營收40%，
約整體營收12%)，其他規格(M6至M8)則是整體營收8%。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  8

南亞CCL具備M4至M9等級量產能力，其中，M4是出貨量最大產
品，主要供應NVIDIA用於RTX 50 系列顯卡。1H26 M6-M8處於
客戶開發與送樣階段 ， 與多位知名終端客戶與CSP客戶積極洽
談大單，預計4Q26協商將陸續完成，並將迎來明顯放量(合約
會要求客戶提供保障資金)，產品組合升級亦帶動營收、獲利
明顯提升 。M9目前則是已具量產能力並進入客戶認證測試中 ，
數據回饋理想且有極小量試賣，預計大批量產時程將落在  
1H27。

CCL自4Q25起CCL每月維持3-5%漲幅，7、8月部分客戶補漲達
10-15%，4Q26將延續逐月調漲步調 。 南亞不放棄利潤依然可觀
且客戶轉單持續湧入的M4，採 「高中低階全線掌握」 ，藉由產
品組合優化大幅推升CCL獲利率 （2Q26 OPM近20%，3Q、4Q26將
進一步追趕目前玻纖的40% OPM水準）。

儘管目前台光電在M9市佔率最高，然南亞的策略是透過開發
M10等高階產品來實現「彎道超車」，一舉超越競爭對手。南
亞的M10採用自製配方，即K2特殊玻纖布搭配HVP4銅箔，以及
自研的碳氫樹脂，不需仰賴昂貴且進口申請繁瑣的石英布方
案，在未來的成本控制與產能擴張上，將具備極佳的競爭優
勢。M10目前則是還在認證中，實測Dk/Df數據已達M10理想水
準且優於同業 ， 因測試需時1-2千小時 。 整體認證持續推進中 ，
主控權在客戶端 ， 希望1H27取得更具體的驗證成果 ， 若認證順
利通過的話，2H27將逐漸放量。

5. 預估3Q26稅後EPS 4.28元，+942.7%YoY 
受惠於AI發展帶動中高階電子材料供應緊缺、價量齊揚，且
中東戰事帶動化工、聚酯、塑膠加工等產品售價隨原料行情
飆升 ， 南亞2Q26營收合計836.5億元 ，+21.9%QoQ，+27.3%YoY，
營收達成率97%， 符合預期 。 營業利益113.6億元 ，+203.5%QoQ，
+772%YoY， 主要受惠於AI產業擴大投資 ， 相關材料需求持續成
長，使電路板等電子材料產品利潤提高。而權益法投資(台塑
化、南亞科、台朔重工、麥寮汽電、台塑烯烴(美國))共認列
獲利190.9億元(原估145億元)， 優於預期 。2Q26歸屬母公司淨
利267.5億元(原估183.2億元)， 獲利達成率146%， 優於預期 ，
稅後EPS 3.37元(原估2.31元)，+87.7%QoQ，YoY虧轉盈。

展望2H26，雖預期3Q26聚酯部門因紡織用途市況不佳 ， 營收看
季減 ， 化工部門營收看持平 ， 然而 ， 塑膠加工部門營收穩健季
增，且公司看好AI算力規模於未來幾年內仍會大幅成長，邊
緣運算將帶動終端電子材料全系列產品升級，高階材料銷售
比重逐漸提高，電子材料將帶動南亞整體營收將持續向上攀
升，預期9月營運表現將優於8月，3Q26優於2Q26，4Q26優於
3Q26。。研究部預估3Q26營收924.3億元(原估877.9億元)，
+10.5%QoQ ，+44%YoY ， 營 業 利 益144.3 億元， +27.1%QoQ ，
+1,293%YoY ，稅後淨利 339.5 億元 ( 原估 268.4 億元 ) ，
+26.9%QoQ，+943%YoY，稅後EPS 4.28元(原估3.38元)。

6. 預估 2026 、2027 年稅後 EPS 13.82 元、 21.64 元 ， 分 別
+2,325%YoY、+56.6%YoY  
展望未來，研究部認為南亞電子材料事業持續增溫，公司整
體營運將進一步轉佳。預估 2026年營收3,416.8億元(原估
3,287.9億元)，+31.5%YoY，毛利率20.4%，+12.1pctYoY。全
年營業利益478.3億元，+1,192%YoY，本業營運明顯回升，加
上權益法認列轉投資子公司獲利大增 ， 預估2026全年稅後EPS 
13.82元(原估12.02元)，+2,325%YoY。

預估2027年營收4,533億元(原估3,788億元)，+32.7%YoY，毛
利率30.9%，+10.5pctYoY。全年營業利益1,099.5億元(原估
922億元)，+129.8%YoY， 本業營運持續提升 ， 權益法認列轉投
資子公司獲利進一步擴大 ， 預估2027全年稅後淨利1,717億元
(原估1,344億元)，+56.6%YoY，EPS 21.64元(原估16.94元)。

評等  定義

## Document 056: 2330台積電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電 群益.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-04
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/陳晏平 TINA.YP.CHEN@uni-psg.com

1H26 主要認列案源為中和 K Tower Y09 商辦案、中和心禾匯住宅
案、台北信義豪宅案持續去化與遞延交屋，1H26 累積營收為 132 億
元 ， 成長31%YOY， 且在整體毛利率較2025 年同期些微提升下 ，1H26
累積稅後淨利為 11.1 億元，成長 61%YOY，EPS 為 1.87 元。展望
2H26，兩個台中新完工建案(合計總銷金額 83 億元)遞延至 4Q26 才
正式推出銷售，且台中市地區為 2026 年以來銷售速度較慢的區域，
預期此兩個台中建案在 2026 年認列案量較少，主要將貢獻在 2027
年。所幸，近幾個月財富效果開始顯現，進而有提升居住品質、資
產配置需求，台北市信義豪宅案 1H26 已認列逾 10 億元，銷售優於
預期 。 因此 ， 研究部預估2026 年合併營收為 268 億元 ，成長 17%YOY，
稅後淨利 20 億元，成長 27%YOY，EPS 為 3.24 元，獲利將自谷底回
升；並預估 2027 年合併營收為 301 億元，成長 12%YOY，稅後淨利
為 24 億元，成長 21%YOY，EPS 為 3.92 元，營運將呈現穩增。投資
建議方面，雖目前房市相關政策已不再緊縮，並朝向逐步鬆綁，加
上高科技產業景氣成長強勁、股市上漲帶來財富效果，使得房市氣
氛將逐漸走出谷底，但因研究部預期冠德 2026~2027 年獲利皆將落
在 3~4 元之譜，未明顯跳增，近兩年現金殖利率亦約當 4~5%，未明
顯高於同業，故維持給予0.5xNAV，目標價40 元，潛在上漲空間有
限，重申中立建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 1H26認列案源主要為帳上成屋持續去化，累積稅後 EPS為1.87
元： 
受惠:(1) 中和K Tower Y09( 原中和秀朗橋捷運聯開商辦案，
總銷金額31億元，銷售率30%)新完工建案開始交屋；(2) 中和
心禾匯(原中和LG08捷運聯開住宅案，總銷金額16億元，銷售
率80%)、 台北市冠德信義豪宅案持續去化與遞延交屋入帳 ；(3)
轉投資根基營造合併營收貢獻大增75%QOQ、52%YOY至61億元，
進而帶動2Q26單季合併 營收明顯成長72%QOQ、57%YOY至84億
元。另在營造子公司有個案結算以及高毛利率冠德信義豪宅案
認列較多下 ， 毛利率亦有所提升，2Q26單季毛利率上揚至28%，
季增4個百分點、年增5個百分點，再扣除費用、所得稅負、根
基少數股權影響，2Q26單季稅後淨利為8億元(歸屬母公司)，
跳增160%QOQ、224%YOY，單季EPS為1.35元。

合計1H26累積營收為132億元，成長31%YOY，且在整體毛利率
較2025年同期些微提升下，1H26累積稅後淨利為11.1億元，成
長61%YOY，EPS為1.87元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 兩個台中新完工建案遞延至4Q26才正式推出銷售， 預估2026年
稅後EPS將自谷底回升至3.24元： 
展望2H26，除帳上成屋案持續去化外，冠德 規劃10~11月將陸
續推出台中裕毛屋住宅案(總銷金額67億元)、 台中G5捷運聯開
商辦案(總銷金額16億元)、台北市萬華直興住宅 案(總銷金額
36億元)，合計新推出案量共119億元。其中，台中裕毛屋住宅
案已於2026年6月取得使照，並有先行潛銷，預計2026年10月
正式推出銷售 ；台中G5捷運聯開商辦案亦已取得使照，計畫
2026年11月開始銷售 ，此兩案皆 將視銷售成績在2H26交屋入
帳。

考量:(1) 台中為2026年以來房屋銷售較慢的區域，且台中裕
毛屋住宅案、台中G5捷運聯開商辦案皆延至4Q26才開始銷售，
預期2026年認列比例將較原先預期少，推估將分別認列 20%、
10%案量；(2) 中和心禾匯、中和K Tower Y09可於2026年順利
銷售並完成交屋25%、1/3案量；(3) 財富效果顯現，進而有提
升居住品質、資產配置需求，預期台北市冠德信義豪宅案可去
化20億元金額，較2025年認列金額倍增。再加計營造、百貨事
業營收貢獻 ， 預估2026年合併營收為268億元 ，成長17%YOY，稅
後淨利20億元，成長27%YOY，EPS為3.24元，將自谷底回升。

3. 2027年將有一台北市住宅案完工 ，加計帳上成屋去化， 預估
2027年稅後EPS將穩增至3.92元： 
展望2027年，除持續銷售前述個案外，預期4Q27還將新推出南
港台電公辦都更住宅案，該案總銷金額達130億元，將視銷售
情況於2028年完工之時認列。而在建案認列方面，預期將有總
銷金額36億元的台北市萬華直興住宅案於3Q27完工 ， 研究部採
保守原則，推估此案在2027年將可順利去化並認列30%案量，
加計:(1)中和心禾匯、中和K Tower Y09、台中裕毛屋住宅案、
台中G5捷運聯開商辦案 ， 推估將分別去化 、 認列15%、1/3、60%、
60%案量 ；(2)台北市冠德信義豪宅案遞延交屋與持續去化20億
元；(3) 營造 、 百貨事業營收貢獻， 預估2027年合併營收為301
億元，成長12%YOY，稅後淨利為24億元，成長21%YOY，EPS為
3.92元，營運將呈現穩增。

4. 帳上土地庫存充足，估計每股資產價值為77元，目前股價評價
約0.5xNAV： 
在扣除2027年完工建案後，帳上土地庫存仍有2.2萬坪，換算
總銷金額為801億元 ， 未來推案無虞 。 就區域分布上 ， 以台北市
佔比最高，高達62%，其次為高雄市、台中市，佔比各為15%、
14%，另有板橋佔比9%。主要個案包含:板橋官邸公辦都更住宅
案、南港台電公辦都更住宅案、台北市行二行三公辦都更案、

5. 預期2027年現金殖利率將在4.4~4.9%區間水準： 
股利政策方面，為保留資本，以提高自有資金比例、降低營運
槓桿，近幾年在正常獲利情況下，現金股利配發率皆落在
35~41%區間水準 。 而因2025年獲利衰退較多 ，2026年配發1.616
元現金股利 ， 且無股票股利 ， 現金股利配發率提升至62%， 已於
2026/8/10除息，除息前現金殖利率4.7%。由於2026年獲利將
自谷底回升，研究部預期2027年現金股利將落在1.6~1.8元區
間水準，換算現金殖利率4.4~4.9%。

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投

Hon Hai (2317.TW): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 3Q 
/ 4Q26E; 3-month revenues preview; Buy (on CL)
24 September 2026 | 8:50PM HKT
We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +14% / +23% QoQ, driven by (1) AI 
data center: The next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp up, with 
higher ASP and early delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first one to deliver the 
latest model, around 2-3 months ahead of peers, driving its Sep and onward revenue 
growth. Followed by GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks 
would fuel the growth in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27, and 
(2) Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along 
with the new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlight in September 
(report link), we reduce global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m to 
1.1bn / 1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; 
however, we remain positive on global leading brands to outperform the market 
given the strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ 
stronger consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect 
premium models to continue gain market share, positive to Hon Hai. Maintain Buy 
(on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 3Q26E revenues to be +40% 
YoY/ +14% QoQ to NT$2.9 trillion. Both Jul and Aug delivered strong revenues YoY 
growth (+54%/ 52% YoY), and we expect to see both YoY and MoM positive growth 
in Sep to Dec, driven by new AI server racks, CPO switch, and latest smartphone 
models and foldable phones. We keep 2026E - 2028E earnings estimates largely 
unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and we continue to 
derive it through near term PE. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 17.9x (vs. 17.8x of our 
previous TP implied 2027E P/E) is derived from peers’ average ratio of trading P/E to 
forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the sum of NI YoY and OPM). Our 
target P/E of 17.9x is in the range of the company’s historical forward P/E since 2010, 
reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from the competitive 
consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which should drive 
profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. Maintain Buy (on 
CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2)

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Compal (2324.TW): AI servers to fuel 4Q26E/1Q27E growth; Aug miss; 
Neutral
27 September 2026 | 6:06PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
considering the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, 
and PC product mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect 
Compal’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 1% / 16% QoQ, riding on AI server racks 
ramp up and the company’s expansion from motherboard (L6) to system (L10 / L11). 
We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, supported by global 
capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, Vietnam and China, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, for which 
demand could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Compal’s Aug revenue was up 15% YoY / down 17% MoM to NT$67bn, or 20% lower 
than our estimate, reflecting PC shipments decline. Aug PC shipments was down 22% 
MoM to 1.4m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising 
memory cost and weak consumer electronics demand.

Earnings revision: Factoring in Compal’s Aug revenue, we cut our 2026-28E net

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$46.1. Our target 
price is based on 14.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 069: 260928_ml_aspeed

### Page 1

Aspeed

Growth profile remains robust into 2027; 
reiterate Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 26,000 TWD | Price: 19,835 TWD

Looming price hike and smoother supply 
Aspeed’s (see report) 4Q26 revenue guidance in our view should incorporate 20% price 
hike across the board including AST2700 BMC (priced at ~US$30 after the hike), mainly 
reflecting the cost inflation from OSAT (outsourced semiconductor assembly/testing) 
vendor. Yet with incremental supply secured across foundry, OSAT, substrate and glass 
cloth, the firm expects a normalized DoI (days of inventory) level of ~12 weeks from 
2Q27.   
Sustained leadership with value-add in security 
Management continues to underscore around 2-year technology lead vs its main 
competitor in the merchant market, while the adoption of Caliptra 2.2 standard in 
AST2750 BMC further enhanced its value-add in network security. We deem this a 
positive to (see report) eMemory given the share gain in PUF business against its 
counterpart in US, from which Aspeed adopts Caliptra 1.1 in AST2700 BMC.   
Rosy demand growth outlook for 2027 
Looking into 2027, Aspeed cites 50-60% growth for BMC applied to GPU and ASIC 
markets, while there could be 1) an even stronger growth in Agentic AI (artificial 
intelligence); and 2) 6-10% YoY in general server (w.o. Agentic AI in our view). Given such 
demand strength, the company may provide an updated version of the BMC TAM (total 
addressable market) by end-2026.   
Flag recent positives from the industry; re-it. Buy 
Recently we see positives to server CPU (central processing unit) supply chain from (see 
reports) Meta’s Muse and expert call takeaways which cite continuous share gain by 
ARM-based solutions. Reiterate Buy on Aspeed given its unrivalled position in a 
structurally growing market.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 6.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Aspeed was founded in 2004 as a fabless company, 
focusing on the design of baseboard management 
controller (BMC), which could be used in remote servers. 
Aspeed has 70+% market share in BMC, while it also offers 
other products including PC/AV extension IC and digital 
image processors.

Investment Rationale 
We rate Aspeed as Buy, as we anticipate stable growth in 
the regular server market into 2026-27, along with its 
growing exposure to AI server market. Outside of server 
BMC, we also like its increasing traction in new products 
(mini-BMC, root of trust, etc.) in the server area. Further, we 
see stronger growth in non-server business led mainly by 
Cupola 360.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 60.5x

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Aspeed (XLKMF) 
Our DCF-based PO is NT$26,000, and we believe the valuation is justified by (1) the 
firm's high market share in the server BMC market, (2) secular growth in overall server 
market, despite near-term ebbs and flows of orders and shipments, (3) nearly 60% 
earnings CAGR in 2026-28E, and (4) about NT$215 net cash/share on hand, we assume 
WACC of 7% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Upside risk to our PO are 
(1) stronger-than-expected operating leverage on stronger revenue growth, and (2) 
faster progress in ramping up non-BMC product lines including Cupola 360.

Downside risks to our PO are 
(1) softer server (thus BMC) demand due to macro uncertainties, or pressured by trade 
conflict 
(2) market-share loss in BMC to current/potential competitors such as Nuvoton 
(3) customers' market-share loss in ones (such as HP and Dell) to which Aspeed has less 
exposure.

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)

4
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

Taiwan ODM/Brands: 3-month preview: Aug +9% MoM on avg; new AI 
server racks and smartphone models to drive growth in coming months
30 September 2026 | 1:20AM HKT
We preview 3-month revenues for 10 companies under coverage in the AI server/PC 
supply chain, and update our estimates for Wistron and Inventec. Across the supply 
chain, we are positive on: (1) Apple’s supply chain on the back of a ramp up of new 
models with form factor changes (i.e. foldable phones in 2026, and the iPhone 20 in 
2027) to support end demand; (2) AI server components to ride on specification 
upgrades; and (3) ASIC AI servers’ rising penetration rate. Buy: Hon Hai, Wiwynn, 
Wistron, AVC; Neutral: Gigabyte, Quanta, Inventec, Compal, ASUS; Sell: Pegatron.  
On a MoM basis, we expect the average revenue growth of the 10 companies to 
come in at +7%/ +2%/ +4% in Sep/ Oct / Nov 2026 (Exhibit 1). We expect 
sequential revenue growth in coming months, driven by the next-generation 
rack-level AI server and new flagship smartphone models shipments ramp up. 
On a YoY basis, we expect the average revenue growth of the 10 companies to 
come in at +49%/ +57%/ +52% in Sep/ Oct / Nov 2026 (Exhibit 2), driven by the 
continuous ramp-up of AI servers, along with a PC product mix upgrade, new 
smartphone models, and high memory costs driving up the selling price.

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 3Q / 4Q26E; 3-month 
revenues preview

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +14% / +23% QoQ, driven by (1) AI data 
center: The next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp up, with higher ASP 
and early delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first one to deliver the latest model, 
around 2-3 months ahead of peers, driving its Sep and onward revenue growth. 
Followed by GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks would fuel 
the growth in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27, and (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlight in September (report link), we 
reduce global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m to 1.1bn / 1.2bn/ 1.2bn 
in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we remain 
positive on global leading brands to outperform the market given the strong brand 
awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger consumption power. We 
remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium models to continue gain 
market share, positive to Hon Hai. Maintain Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 3Q26E revenues to be +40% YoY/ 
+14% QoQ to NT$2.9 trillion. Both Jul and Aug delivered strong revenues YoY growth 
(+54%/ 52% YoY), and we expect to see both YoY and MoM positive growth in Sep to 
Dec, driven by new AI server racks, CPO switch, and latest smartphone models and 
foldable phones. We keep 2026E - 2028E earnings estimates largely unchanged.

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): New AI server rack ramp up to support 2H26 growth; PC slowdown; 
Neutral

With a high base in 1H26, driven by PC consumption pull-in and AI server rack 
expansion, we expect flat QoQ in 3Q26 / 4Q26 at 2% / 2%, reflecting slowdown in PC 
shipment and AI server racks facing model transitions. The company’s PC shipment 
declined by 30% / 37% YoY in Jul / Aug, reflecting the impact of PC consumption pull-in 
in 1H26 and slow PC shipment under high memory costs. We expect revenues to 
sequentially increase in Oct to Dec and further accelerate to 54% MoM in Dec, reflecting 
the ramp up of next generation AI server racks, which carry higher ASP . With strong AI 
spending driving not only AI servers (GPU and ASIC) but also general servers, along with 
customer requirement on capacity diversification, we estimate Quanta’s capex to stay at 
a high level of NT$44bn / NT$32bn in 2026 / 27E (vs. NT$15bn in 2025). In Sep, Quanta 
announced factory rental in USA at 199k square feet (US$368k per month for 126

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling rising adoption along with new AI servers across GPU and ASIC to 
drive sequential monthly revenues growth; Buy

3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue sequential 
growth through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +18% / +27% QoQ, 
driven by (1) new AI server racks ramp up, driving overall liquid cooling penetration rate. 
As we highlighted in June (report link), we expect AI server rack (NVL72-equivalent) 
shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 55k/ 105k/ 163k in 2026-28E, increasing by 
16% / 20% in 2027 / 28E, reflecting our positive view on AI server racks, (2) its major 
ASIC AI server customer’s new model ramp up, driving its air-cooling and liquid-cooling 
components, along with a rising liquid cooling penetration rate, (3) its customer base 
expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further drive its liquid cooling components in 
4Q26, and (4) increasing adoption rate of ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit the 
lightweight design and major customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s net 
income to grow at a +39% CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a 
growing liquid cooling penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total 
solutions (air cooling, liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,513 on a target P/E multiple of 30.3x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition;

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion; Aug strong beat; Buy

Wiwynn delivered strong Aug revenues, which were 27% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general servers demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI servers ramping up. We expect sequential revenues growth to continue 
through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +46% / +22% QoQ, driven 
by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. Maintain Buy with TP 
of NT$3,777 (21.5x 2027E P/E).

Ongoing capacity expansion: As we highlighted in Aug (report link), Wiwynn 
announced a 2H26 capex budget of US$942m, implying 2026 capex could be up to 
NT$40bn (US$1.3bn). On Sep 16, the company announced equipment procurement of 
US$16m (NT$512m), reflecting the strong AI demand and the company’s commitments 
on capacity expansion. We estimate the company’s capex at NT$44bn in 2026E, and to 
stay at a similar high level through 2028E, supporting its production sites diversification 
across the US, Mexico, and Malaysia, AI servers expansion from ASIC to GPU-powered, 
and products expansion across different ASICs and GPUs, along with further customers 
penetration.

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): Aug strong beat on rack-level AI server; Buy with TP raised to NT$295

Wistron’s Aug revenue was up 166% YoY to NT$460bn, or 48% higher than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, offsetting the impact of 
AI server model transition and sluggish PC end demand. Aug PC shipment was down 
13% MoM to 1.4m units, mainly on the 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising memory 
cost. We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up across GPU, CPU, and LPU. The 
company plans to invest US$209m to expand its AI server capacity in US and Vietnam 
(link), which would further support its revenue growth in coming years, in our view. 
Maintain Buy.

Earnings revision: We factor in Wistron’s Aug revenue and revise up our 2026-28E net 
income by 2% / 3% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 
11% / 7% / 6%, mainly on higher rack-level AI server shipments and ASP , considering the 
strong end demand and rising memory cost. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised down 
mainly on higher contribution from rack-level AI server, which usually carries lower GM 
than other product lines. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are largely unchanged.

low base in Sep and the potential raw material supply recovery, leading our 3Q / 4Q26E 
revenues up 45% / 47% YoY. We remain positive on Inventec’s AI server business 
expansion, while the component short supply may weigh on the near term growth. 
Maintain Neutral.

Earnings revision: We factor in Inventec’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
income by 5% / 4% / 2%, mainly on higher revenues. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 
8% / 10% / 6%, considering the rising AI server ASP on the rising component costs. Our 
2026-28E GMs and opex ratios are largely unchanged.

### Page 15

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$61.0. Our target 
price is based on 15.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.  
Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix upgrade

3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, mainly 
driven by AI server racks ramp up with higher ASP and major smartphone brand 
customer’s new model launches (report link). Overall, we project Pegatron’s 3Q/4Q26E 
revenues to grow +18%/+25% YoY, driven by: (1) new model launches from a major 
smartphone customer supporting September and 4Q26 revenue growth; (2) a 
continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments, across rack-level and baseboard-based, 
riding on the growing demand in Neocloud and enterprises, (3) broadband, IoT, and 
game console entering peak season. We are positive on Pegatron’s expansion from 
consumer electronics to AI servers and automotive electronics, while the high memory 
costs could cap PC and smartphone market growth. Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Aug revenue was up 34% YoY / 3% MoM to NT$88bn, or 12% lower than our 
estimate. This reflects that high memory costs continue to cap PC and smartphone 
growth, despite a strong ramp-up in AI servers. Aug PC shipments was down 49% YoY to 
0.40-0.45m, reflecting the impact of consumption pull in under the high memory costs. 
Its major smartphone brand customer launched new models in Sep, supporting 
Pegatron’s growth in Sep and 4Q26; prior to September, the company suffered from 
model transition (i.e. declining demand for older models while new models were in a 
slow shipment ramp-up phase).

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this

E Ink (8069.TWO): E-paper application continues to expand; expect 
modest consumer electronics mix; Aug beat; Neutral
30 September 2026 | 3:59PM HKT
We remain positive on growing e-paper applications across e-readers, e-notes, and 
e-labels. However, consumer electronics end-demand could remain modest amid 
high memory costs. We expect E Ink’s consumer electronics (e-readers, e-notes) to 
decline 13% YoY in 2026E (vs. +6% YoY in 2025), and recover back to +12-16% YoY 
in 2027E-28E; on IoT applications (ESL and Signage), we expect revenue to grow at a 
+32% CAGR in 2026E-28E (vs. +22% YoY in 2025), driven by rising adoption on 
savings of labour costs, electricity, and paper, digitalization trend, and growing ESG 
awareness. Signage new products could also drive revenue growth with high ASPs, 
despite lower GMs considering in module format to accelerate customers’ adoption. 
Our target P/E multiple declines to 11x (vs. 13x previous TP implied 2027E P/E) on 
consumer electronics sector de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m TP is 
NT$142 (vs. NT$153 previously). Maintain Neutral.

3-month revenues preview: We expect a sequential increase in revenues through 
Sep-Dec, driven by e-labels, Signage ramp up, and better consumer electronics 
seasonality. The ongoing digitalization should support e-paper adoption; E Ink’s 
e-labels further penetrated in Canada, serving Sobeys (retail) to deploy ESLs across 
300 to 350 stores over the next 18 months (link). Overall, we expect 3Q26E / 4Q26E 
revenues to grow at +0% / +16% QoQ on the mix of modest consumer electronics 
end-demand under high memory costs, and ongoing e-labels adoption and Signage 
shipment ramp up.

### Page 2

Earnings revision

We factor in 2Q26 and Aug results and revise up our 2026E-28E net income by 15% / 
10% / 8% mainly on higher gross margins, given better product mix (e-labels mainly 
delivered via e-paper while consumer electronics mainly via module, carry a lower GM). 
Overall, we expect 2026E revenues growth at +13% YoY (vs. +12% YoY in 2025A), driven 
by e-labels growing application and adoption, which we model to grow +47% YoY (vs. 
+22% YoY in 2025A), while we also expect a decline in consumer electronics under high 
memory costs (-13% YoY vs. +6% YoY in 2025). We continue to expect revenue growth in 
2027E-28E driven by (1) rising color e-notes / e-readers enlarging size, (2) more stores 
adopting e-labels to save operation costs with growing outdoor signage. On GM, we 
increase our forecasts in 2026E-28E to reflect 2Q26 beat on better product mix.

### Page 3

Valuation

We continue to derive our 12m TP based on a near-term P/E. We derive our target P/E 
via E Ink’s avg. 12m forward PEG ratio since 2017. With largely the same PEG ratio of 
0.5x, 22% forward year earnings growth (vs. 26% previously), our new target P/E is at 
11x (vs. 13x our TP implied 2027E P/E previously). The new target P/E of 11x is largely in 
line with the company’s avg.-1stv. P/E, reflecting the consumer electronics sector 
de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m target price is NT$142 (vs. NT$153 
previously). Maintain Neutral.

### Page 4

Price target methodology and risks - E Ink 
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral rated on E Ink. Our 12m target price for E Ink is 
based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. Our 12-m 
TP of NT$142 is based on a target P/E multiple of 11x forward year EPS (2027E). Our 
target P/E multiple of 11x is derived from the company’s avg. PEG since 2017 (0.5x), as 
we expect macro uncertainties to reduce the visibility of e-paper adoption, while we 
remain positive in the long term: technology upgrades (colour, larger sizes) to unlock the 
e-paper market potential. 
Upside / Downside risks: (1) Faster- / slower-than-expected e-readers / e-notes 
penetration rate, (2) Faster- / slower-than-expected ESL adoption rate, (3) Fiercer- / 
healthier-than-expected competition, which could be driven by competition from other 
technology gadgets (e.g. AI / AR glasses, AI PCs, tablets, smart watches, etc.) or new 
entrants in e-paper industry.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

## Document 088: 261002_daiwa_nypcb

Source PDF: all report pdf/261002_daiwa_nypcb.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: NYPCB released its August results (for pre-tax profit and net profit) after 
market hours on 2 October mainly due to recent volatile share price movements. Net profit 
in August 2026 came in at TWD1,092m (EPS: TWD1.69), up 665% YoY, with pre-tax profit 
at TWD1,486m (pre-tax margin at 27.8%). The August pre-tax margin of 27.8% is close to 
our current forecast (28.5%/32.9% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). As we expect limited impact from 
non-ops and on opex in August, we infer the gross margin in August should have reached 
to c.30-31% (vs. our forecast c.30.3/34.4% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). This echoes our previous 
notes (Spec upgrade to drive additional ABF substrate demand); we expect NYPCB’s 
gross margin will recover to c.30% after Kunshan’s yield rate improvement in August. As 
such, we believe the August results to be largely on track with market expectations. 
However, we believe the share price upside will come from BT substrate, where we should 
see better progress in its implementing price hikes now (vs. limited visibility earlier in 
September). We expect NYPCB to raise ASP of BT substrates by single digits QoQ in 
4Q26 and +20% QoQ in 1Q27. This is aligned with our previous comment (Clarification on 
“no intention to hike price from 4Q26”) that we expect BT substrate prices will accelerate 
again in 2027 despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E. We view NYPCB’s 
August results announcement as a near-term price catalyst. We have a Buy rating (1) on 
NYPCB with a 12-month TP of TWD2,460. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand 
for switches/PCs/ smartphones.

## Document 093: 261005_gs_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261005_gs_TSMC.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

TSMC will host its 3Q26 analyst meeting on 15 October. We expect a 
strong finish to 2026 with a sustained growth outlook into 2027 for 
TSMC as demand broadens across AI GPUs/AI ASICs, networking 
and server CPUs. In particular, the stronger CPU demand driven by 
agentic AI represents a key change over the past year. We forecast 
2026E/2027E revenue growth of 42.0%/36.9% YoY (USD terms), 
with GM at 67.2%/67.5%. We also raise our 2027E/2028E CapEx 
forecasts to US$85bn/US$98bn (vs. US$78bn/US$82bn 
previously) as we expect TSMC to further ramp up its capacity 
investment to support longer-term customer demand. 
Strong sequential growth into 4Q26 with modest N2 ramp dilution 
We expect TSMC’s 3Q26E revenue to grow 15.3% QoQ, followed 
by a further 11.0% QoQ increase in 4Q26E (both in USD terms), 
supported by continued strength in AI/HPC demand. On 
profitability, we model GM edging down to 67.5%/67.3% in 
3Q26E/4Q26E, vs. 67.7% in 2Q26, reflecting dilution from the 
ongoing N2 ramp.  
Momentum sustaining into 2027, supported by broader AI demand 
We expect TSMC’s revenue to grow 36.9% YoY (vs. 32.0% previously) 
in USD terms in 2027E, supported by sustained demand for AI 
accelerators and networking, alongside an increasingly strong 
contribution from server CPUs. At our Communacopia + Technology 
conference, management highlighted that lower token costs should 
stimulate greater AI consumption, while discussions with customers 
and US CSPs has reinforced confidence in demand and 
infrastructure readiness. We believe these trends support continued 
strength in leading-edge demand, particularly for both N2 and N3, 
where management expects supply to remain insufficient despite 
further expansion.

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

On the supply side, we expect capacity additions, N5-to-N3 conversion, and 
productivity gains to support higher wafer output, while continued CoWoS expansion 
helps enable associated wafer shipments. We model GM improving modestly to 67.5% in 
2027E (vs. 67.2% in 2026E), supported by higher pricing, favorable product mix, 
sustained high utilization and continued productivity gains. 
Higher CapEx to support longer-term expansion 
We maintain our 2026E CapEx forecast at US$64bn, while raising our 2027E/2028E 
forecasts to US$85bn/US$98bn (vs. US$78bn/US$82bn previously), to incorporate 1) 
cost inflation from equipment vendors, and 2) initial spending associated with the 
potential new Texas expansion; however, we expect the new site to only enter mass 
production beyond 2032. For capacity, we continue to model our end-2027E/2028E 
N3/N2 capacity at 200kwpm/140kwpm and 220kwpm/200kwpm, respectively. 
Other key areas in focus for the upcoming analyst meeting 
Long-term AI growth outlook: We believe the broadening of AI demand across 
accelerators, CPUs and networking further supports TSMC’s long-term growth 
opportunity. We would look for updated comments from management on the key 
assumptions underpinning its AI outlook through 2030, where demand drivers have 
strengthened most over the past year, and the factors that could materially change this 
outlook. 
Longer-term CapEx outlook and US expansion: With customer demand continuing to 
drive investment in leading-edge capacity and advanced packaging, we would look for 
management to provide further direction on its longer-term CapEx trajectory and the 
demand visibility supporting these investment decisions. We would also welcome 
updated comments on whether customer demand could lead to further expansion in the 
US beyond current Arizona site, and the potential timing and scale of such investments. 
Foundry competition and technology leadership: We would look for management’s 
perspective on the evolving competitive landscape, including Intel, Samsung and 
Terafab, and the implications for TSMC’s longer-term positioning. In particular, we would 
welcome further discussion of its technology leadership, and whether management 
expects this gap to widen or narrow across upcoming process generations.

Earnings estimate revisions, valuation and risks

Forecast changes 
We have revised up 2026/27/28E earnings by 1%/7%/8% to reflect 1) higher GM 
assumptions as we see a milder GM dilution from N2 ramp, and 2) higher UTRs especially 
for N2/N3 given stronger demand from AI/HPC.

Reiterate Buy; TP raise to NT$3,300 from NT$3,100 
We raise our 12m TP to NT$3,300 from NT$3,100 (2330.TW), following our upward 
earnings revisions. Our TP is based on a target P/E of 22x (unchanged, benchmarked to 
1.0stdv above 5-year avg. forward P/E) applied to our higher 2027E EPS. Accordingly, we 
raise our 12 month TP for the ADR to US$660 from US$620, based on an unchanged FX

### Page 7

Investment Thesis - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
TSMC is a leading global foundry company specializing in cutting-edge nodes. Its leading 
technology stance enables it to enjoy more than 60% revenue share in the global 
foundry market. We like TSMC as we believe its solid technology leadership and 
execution better position it vs. peers to capture the industry’s long-term structural 
growth opportunities, particularly in areas such as AI/5G/HPC/EV. In addition, valuation 
looks attractive in our view, with the shares trading at the mid-range of their 10-year 
trading history (P/E: 10-29x). Furthermore, we view TSMC as the key AI enabler among 
our Taiwan Semis coverage, thanks to its leadership stance in leading edge nodes and 
advanced packaging technology – CoWoS (chip on wafer on substrate). We believe TSMC 
will achieve its 25% revenue CAGR target for the next several years, driven primarily by 
increasing silicon content growth and AI/HPC demand, with LT GM to remain at 56%+. 
We are Buy rated.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
Valuation methodology: We have a 12m TP of NT$3,300, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12m TP of 
US$660, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. 
Key downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery 
impacting capacity utilization; (2) slower customer node migrations; (3) slowdown in AI 
investment resulting in lower long-term semiconductor content growth; (4) poor 
yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Increasing power consumption, along with the deployment of
BBU/CBU/800/±400VDC/SST  in AI clusters, could push the
technology barrier higher and present growth opportunities for
players with superior capabilities. Fuel cells could also emerge

as a new opportunity amid power constraints.
Secular demand for power amid AI capex: AI-related power consumption will
continue to increase, and we believe this will lead to secular demand for power-
related components, such as power supply units (PSUs), battery backup units
(BBUs), capacitor bank units (CBUs), coolant distribution units (CDUs), and so on.
Moreover, the requirement for efficiency will inevitably necessitate the adoption of
800/±400 voltage direct current (VDC)  in the power rack/center/block

configuration (initially from late 2026 and broadly in 2028, we estimate), eventually
extending to the deployment of  solid state transformers (SST) in the longer term –
all pointing to a more centralized power delivery architecture in the future. This
trend of aggregation could raise the technology barriers amid the fusion of white/
grey   space, favoring players with better capabilities and execution. Overall, we
forecast the datacenter PSU market size to grow at a 75% three-year CAGR to reach

US$33bn in 2028.
Fuel cells to improve flexibility and resilience of AI power delivery: Despite the
strong AI capex outlook (MSe global cloud capex +97%/20% YoY in 2026/2027),
power has become a bottleneck, indicated by MS's forecast power shortfall of 5GW
in 2026, scaling to a total 72GW through 2029 (see report). Fuel cells, among other
alternative energy sources, could emerge as a good solution to pick up some slack
and enhance efficiency through active micro grid management. Those who are able

to offer comprehensive solutions from power sources to power delivery could be
increasingly more competitive in the market.
Assuming coverage of Delta and Lite-On:
• Delta (OW, PT NT$2,500, based on 30x 2027/2028 P/E) should maintain its
dominant position in the industry with its integrated power/thermal related
solutions while fuel cells could add incremental growth opportunities in the
longer term. We think a 30x P/E target multiple is justified by our estimated

63% earnings CAGR in 2025-28, driven by a 44% revenue CAGR and
operating margin expansion to 22.1% in 2028 vs 15.1% in 2025,
• Lite-On (EW, PT NT$315, based on 17x 2027/2028 P/E), the second-largest
player in AI power-related products, should also ride the tailwinds despite its
slower progress  in HVDC power racks and liquid cooling solutions. We think
a 17x P/E  target multiple is justified by our estimated 47% earnings CAGR in
2025-28 (29% revenue CAGR and 6.8ppt OPM expansion  to 16.8% in 2028).

Executive Summary
AI cluster performance evolving to fulfill  demand
The rapid adoption of artificial intelligence is driving a significant increase in global data
center compute demand. Advances in foundation models, reasoning capabilities, agentic
AI, and enterprise AI deployment are increasing the computational resources required for
both training and inference. To support this growth, hyperscalers and model developers

continue to deploy larger and more powerful AI clusters, driving demand for greater
compute, memory, and networking capacity.
As AI infrastructure scales, cluster performance is increasingly determined by the
efficiency of the entire system rather than the capabilities of individual processors alone.
Compute, networking, cooling, and power infrastructure must all evolve together to
support higher rack densities and larger deployments. While semiconductor innovation

remains critical, supporting infrastructure is becoming an equally important factor in
enabling future AI growth.
AI cluster performance is fundamentally a power problem
As AI clusters continue to scale in size and performance, power consumption is becoming
one of the most important constraints on future infrastructure deployment. The power
challenge is no longer limited to GPUs alone, as modern AI clusters comprise tightly
integrated compute, networking, memory, storage, and cooling systems, all of which

require increasing amounts of power as performance requirements rise. Consequently, the
next phase of AI infrastructure investment should be viewed not only as a semiconductor
upgrade cycle but also as a broad-based power infrastructure upgrade cycle.
Server power consumption has increased significantly across successive AI hardware
generations. Nvidia's architecture roadmap illustrates the industry's migration from
relatively modest server-level power budgets under the Ampere and Hopper generations

toward rack-scale systems under Blackwell and future Rubin architectures. Estimated rack-
level power consumption has increased from roughly 40kW for Hopper systems to
approximately 120kW for GB200 NVL72 and 140kW for GB300 NVL72, with future Vera
Rubin systems expected to approach 200-300kW per rack with current Vera Rubin
systems reaching up to 330kW per rack while future Vera Rubin generations are expected
to surpass these levels. As compute density continues to increase, power delivery

infrastructure is becoming a critical enabler of system performance.
The increase in power consumption is not limited to compute hardware. Networking
systems are also becoming increasingly power intensive as switch bandwidth scales from
100G architectures toward 800G and 1.6T generations. Despite substantial process-node
improvements, switch ASIC power consumption has continued to rise due to increasing
complexity and higher SerDes speeds. As a result, networking equipment now requires

significantly larger power budgets than previous generations, creating additional demand
for higher-capacity power supplies throughout the data center.

### Page 4

M
Asia Pacific Insight

The growth in power consumption is driving a corresponding upgrade cycle for power-
supply units (PSUs). Earlier AI server generations typically relied on multiple 3-3.3KW
power supplies installed directly within individual server chassis. As the industry
transitions toward rack-scale architectures, power conversion is increasingly centralized
into dedicated power shelves capable of supporting substantially larger power loads. In
Blackwell systems, power shelves utilize 5.5KW power supplies, while future Rubin-

generation platforms are expected to increase PSU ratings to approximately 18KW,
representing a significant increase in both PSU content and power density.
Delivering higher power within a similar footprint creates significant engineering
challenges. Increasing PSU power density requires advances across multiple areas,
including higher-performance power semiconductors such as silicon carbide (SiC) and
gallium nitride (GaN), more efficient power-conversion topologies, smaller and more

capable magnetic components, improved thermal-management solutions, and greater
system integration. As output power increases, thermal constraints become increasingly
important, making heat dissipation, airflow optimization, and mechanical design critical
determinants of PSU performance.
Consequently, on top of the volume driven growth, the power-supply industry is
benefiting from the technology driven dynamics, where efficiency, power density, and

thermal performance are becoming key competitive differentiators. As AI rack power
requirements continue to increase, PSU innovation will become an increasingly important
component of the broader AI infrastructure investment cycle.

CAGR of 75% to reach US$33bn in 2028 from US$6bn in 2025, driven mainly by server
shipment growth, increasing power consumption per rack, and the adoption of HVDC

power racks from 2027.

Complementary hardware for more resilient power delivery
As AI clusters become more power intensive, maintaining stable and resilient power
delivery is emerging as a critical design priority. Traditional centralized power

architectures are increasingly being complemented by localized energy-storage and
power-conditioning technologies that can respond rapidly to dynamic workload demands.
Battery Backup Units (BBUs) represent one of the most important additions to modern AI
power architectures. Positioned close to the computing workload, BBUs provide localized
energy storage capable of responding significantly faster than facility-level backup
systems. Their role extends beyond conventional backup power functionality. BBUs can

### Page 6

M
Asia Pacific Insight
6
absorb transient fluctuations, smooth power ramps, and improve overall rack-level power
stability. By providing local buffering capacity, BBUs help reduce the burden on upstream
power distribution infrastructure while improving overall system resilience.
Exhibit 11: Concept of peak shaving for AI server power loads
Source: Nvidia
Exhibit 12: BBU
Source: Panasonic
Capacitor Backup Units (CBUs) address an even shorter-duration segment of the power

stability challenge. Utilizing supercapacitors and other high-power-density storage
technologies, CBUs can deliver and absorb power within milliseconds, making them
particularly effective for managing rapid GPU power spikes. These systems act as shock
absorbers within the electrical architecture, preventing sudden load changes from
propagating throughout the broader power network. As AI rack power densities continue
increasing, capacitor-based energy storage is becoming an attractive solution for

improving power quality without requiring substantial oversizing of facility infrastructure.
Exhibit 13: CBU
Source: Panasonic
Exhibit 14: GB300 power shelf with integrated capacitors
Source: Nvidia
Together, BBUs and CBUs provide complementary benefits. BBUs are designed to handle
longer-duration events, while CBUs are optimized for extremely fast transient response.
The integration of these technologies enables operators to improve power quality, reduce

stranded infrastructure capacity, enhance system reliability, and increase effective
utilization of expensive electrical assets. As rack densities continue to rise, localized
energy-storage solutions are likely to become a standard component of AI data center
design rather than an optional enhancement.

### Page 7

M
Asia Pacific Insight
Morgan Stanley Research 7
The adoption of BBU and CBU architectures may also enable future increases in rack
density. As operators attempt to push toward several hundred kilowatts per rack,
localized power-buffering solutions can help mitigate transient peaks and reduce stress on
grid-facing infrastructure. This allows operators to deploy higher-density computing
resources without proportionately increasing upstream electrical capacity.

HVDC emerging as a necessity, with SST in the pipeline
800VDC and ±400VDC progressing simultaneously
The rapid increase in AI rack power density is creating growing challenges for conventional
power-distribution architectures. As rack-level power consumption rises from tens of
kilowatts to several hundreds of KWs, electrical losses, cable size, thermal management

requirements, and overall system complexity increase significantly. Under traditional low-
voltage power architectures, delivering larger amounts of power requires proportionally
higher current, which increases resistive losses and places greater stress on cables,
connectors, busbars, and power-conversion equipment.
At the same time, every power-conversion stage introduces efficiency losses and
generates additional heat. As AI deployments scale into multi-megawatt and eventually

gigawatt-level campuses, even small efficiency improvements can translate into
meaningful reductions in operating costs, cooling requirements, and infrastructure
investment. Consequently, the industry is increasingly focused on power architectures
that can reduce conversion stages, lower current levels, and improve overall power-
delivery efficiency.
Against this backdrop, high-voltage direct current (HVDC) is emerging as one of the most
important architectural upgrades for next-generation AI infrastructure. By increasing

distribution voltage, HVDC allows the same amount of power to be delivered at a lower
current, reducing conduction losses and improving system efficiency. Higher-voltage
architectures also improve scalability for ultra-high-density rack deployments by
alleviating some of the physical and thermal constraints associated with conventional
power-distribution systems.

### Page 8

M
Asia Pacific Insight
8
Exhibit 16: Better power efficiency for 800VDC architecture
Source: Delta
Exhibit 17: Better efficiency leads to cost savings
Source: Delta
Nvidia's roadmap illustrates the industry's gradual migration toward higher-voltage power
architectures. Earlier AI server generations primarily utilized conventional power-delivery
schemes centered around rack-level 48V distribution. As rack power requirements
increased with Blackwell-generation systems, industry participants began evaluating

higher-voltage architectures capable of supporting substantially greater power density.
Looking ahead, the industry is increasingly converging around 800VDC as a potential
next-generation standard for large-scale AI deployments, particularly as rack-level power
consumption approaches several hundred kilowatts. The transition reflects the need to
improve power-distribution efficiency while supporting the continued growth of AI
computing infrastructure.

We believe that the adoption of the 800VDC power rack could start from late 4Q26,
though it should be small scope deployment through 2027, since the in-rack power shelf
is the reference design in the Vera Rubin NVL72 rack. Broader adoption should happen
more likely from late 2027 and 2028, as the Vera Rubin Ultra rack further pushes up the
power consumption.
Exhibit 18: HVDC deployment roadmap
Source: Nvidia

### Page 9

M
Asia Pacific Insight
Morgan Stanley Research 9
The 800VDC power rack BOM is primarily driven by the PSU, which accounts for 50–55%
of total cost, followed by the BBU at 25–30%, with other components comprising the
remaining 15–20%. This cost structure reflects the broader functionality integrated into an
HVDC power rack, combining power conversion, backup power and supporting
components within a centralized architecture.
For power supply players, one of the most direct implications is that an HVDC power rack

carries around 4–5x higher dollar content per watt compared with a conventional in-rack
PSU solution, since the former integrates more components into a robust system and
power supply players usually ship the entire rack instead of just the components.
Exhibit 19: BOM cost breakdown for an 800VDC power rack
